# Fields expected per kind (cache/wire set aside):
#   E: st (or seed) leaves 400/500; store, shape, srv, hist, ev, v1read, ident change with it.
#   M: store n=1 -> n=2; shape -> not-written,new; srv, v1read, ident change; st/ev/hist may change with the count.
#   I: ident only (kept/null/num/empty/strN -> absent; a v3-written record -> its computed identifier, uuid).
# Not moving, and why (departures from the brief's expectations):
#   - v3 idundef (`_id:"undefined"` through API v3) stays 400: refuseClientId refuses any client _id before the
#     identifier step, and v3 has no UUID_HANDLING move, so ID-Q6 never sees the value. Only the seed's ident moves
#     in the v1/inproc x v3 cross cells (kind I).
#   - idundef twoapp cells where `_id:"undefined"` stays the _id (socket, v1 food/activity/profile/devicestatus) keep
#     one record: that is an _id match (duplicate key, replace or merge by _id), not an identifier match. Listed in
#     idundef-twoapp-one-record.txt.
#   - empty and jsonnull on API v3: v3 already computed an identifier for them; no field moves (log line only).
#   - len256, urlsafe, colon, slash, nonascii: identifiers; no move. None of the 1,929 older cells moves.
